# Operations Research: question papers in full

101 unique papers · 2006-07 to 2025-26 · Computer Applications (MCA), Computer Engineering, Mechanical, Information Technology, Chemical, Computer Science and Business Systems, Mechatronics, Data Science, Civil, Common (all branches)

Each paper: year, exam, semester, departments and programmes, then its full text as extracted. Scanned papers were read by OCR, so equations and symbols may contain recognition errors. Structured data: [`questions.jsonl`](questions.jsonl) (one record per question) and [`papers.jsonl`](papers.jsonl) (one record per paper). Index of files: [`README.md`](README.md).

An Air Force is experimenting with three types of bombs P, Q and R in which three
kinds of explosives namely A, B and C will be used. Taking the various factors in
the account, it has been decided to use at the maximum of 600 kg of-explosive A, at

2. 2 kg, bomb Q requires 1, 4, 3 kg and bomb R requires 4, 2, 3 kg of explosives A,

and Y require the same production capacity. The firm has a permanent contract to supply at least 2
tonnnes of X and at least 3 tonnes of Y per day to another company. Each tonne of X requires 20
machine hours of production time and each tonne of Y requires 50 machine hours of production
time. The daily maximum possible number of machine hours is 360.All of the firm’s output can be
sold. The profit made is Rs. 80/- per tonne of X and Rs .120/- per tonne of Y. Formulate this problem
as LP model and solve it by using graphical method to determine the production schedule that
yields maximum profit.

Q5 B   Amechanic repairs four machines. The mean time between service requirements is 5 hours for
each machine and forms an exponential distribution. The mean repair time is one hour and also
follows the same distribution pattern. Machine downtime costs Rs. 25/- per hour and mechanic
costs Rs.55/- per day. Determine the following:

(a) Probability that the service facility will be idle.
(b) The probability of various number of machines (o through 4) to be out of order and being
repaired.
(c) Expected number of machines waiting to be repaired.
(d) Expected downtime cost per day.
Would it be economical to engage two mechanics ( It is assumed that each mechanic with his 2
machines constitute a separate system with no interaction ) , each repairing only two machines?

would result in a return of either 5% or the 8% on the sum invested with probabilities 0.45 and 0.55 (Assume all

men-days of work and yields a profit of Rs. 20. If farmer has Rs. 1,00,000 for plantation

method.

production of each A requires 6 hours of machine time and 4 hours of assembly time,

castings per day . What quantities of each battery to be made to maximize the profit?
